									document.getElementById( event.slot.getSlotElementId() ).innerHTML = '\r\n<div class="ipsKursLoader" data-ipsKursVisible data-ipsKursLoad data-ipsKursLoad-url="' + ips.getSetting( 'baseURL' ) + '?app=articles&module=articles&controller=ajax&do=listWithPhotos&limit=4" data-ipsKursLoad-type="get" data-ipsKursLoad-csrf="false"></div>';
									
									$( document ).trigger( 'contentChange', [ $( '#' + event.slot.getSlotElementId() ) ] );
								}
							}
						} );
					}
				}
			} );
		} );
	}
	
	$( window ).one( 'ips.trackingBlocked', { elem: $( '#KD-Content1' ) }, function( e )
	{
		e.data.elem
      		.html( "<a href='//meteobar.com' target='_blank' class='ipsKursBlock ipsType_center ipsSpacer_bottom' style='display: block; width: 728px; height: 90px; background-image: url(https://old.kurs.com.ua/static/css/images/mb728x90.png?1)'></a>" );
		$( document ).trigger( 'contentChange', [ e.data.elem ] );
	} );
} )(this);
</script>
